- **arXiv**: [2607.23733](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23733) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Foster Provost, Panos Ipeirotis
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.LG; econ.GN; stat.AP
- **Tags**: Decision Making, AI Economics
- **Summary**: 企业选择AI项目时难以评估回报。本文提出eROI框架，将项目拆分为成功价值、成功可能性和投资需求三个独立评估组件。在Compass公司案例中，该框架有效区分了高价值项目与应搁置项目，避免了仅依赖ROI的误判。

<h3 id="324">[324] Escaping the Euclidean Void: Manifold-Informed Flow Matching for Sequential Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23762](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23762) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dengzhao Fang, Jingtong Gao, Yu Li, Xiangyu Zhao, Yi Chang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Flow Matching, Graph Neural Network
- **Summary**: 连续生成推荐中的流匹配路径可能穿过无语义意义的欧几里得空洞。本文提出MIRAGE方法，利用物品共现图作为语义流形，将插值路径状态对齐到局部锚点，仅训练时使用图，推理一步完成。在四个数据集上，MIRAGE一致超越现有方法，尤其提升稀疏目标推荐性能。

<h3 id="325">[325] WISERouter: LLM Routing with Workload Budget Constraint</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23765](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23765) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yifei Li, Zihui Gao, Laks V.S. Lakshmanan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, Multi-Armed Bandit, Contextual Bandits
- **Summary**: 现有LLM路由方法难以在预算约束下自适应分配或需要大量标注数据。本文提出WISERouter框架，将路由建模为约束上下文多臂老虎机，支持离线学习和在线探索，并证明在线版本具有T平方根次遗憾界。在RouterBench和SWE-Bench上，WISERouter在固定预算下性能更优且严格满足约束。

<h3 id="326">[326] Outcome-Fair Restless Multi-Armed Bandits for Stochastic Deadline Scheduling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23772](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23772) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shakti Sharma, Rahul Meshram
- **Subjects**: eess.SY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Armed Bandit, Fairness, Scheduling
- **Summary**: 经典RMAB调度问题最大化回报但忽视公平性。本文引入结果公平的Whittle指数策略，通过虚拟队列动态保障各组完成率。数值实验表明，该策略在公平性指标上显著优于无公平和输入公平策略，但有一定收益损失，且随服务器容量增加而减小。

<h3 id="327">[327] Scale Weight Decay and Train Better</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23777](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23777) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anuj Apte
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: Optimization, Training Efficiency, Learning Rate Scheduling
- **Summary**: 标准权重衰减导致网络权重不断缩小，可能偏离最优。本文提出按峰值学习率比例缩放权重衰减，证明其保持渐近平稳性且不引入额外偏差。在MoE模型训练中，缩放权重衰减使Muon优化器达到相同验证损失的速度加快30%，且权重范数稳定。

<h3 id="328">[328] A Few Words Go a Long Way: Language Guided Robot Policy Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23784](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23784) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daphne Chen, Archit Ritesh Jain, Eric Goossen, Emma Romig, Michael Murray, Nick Walker, Maya Cakmak
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Program Synthesis, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 端到端机器人策略难以解释和修正。本文提出ARCHITECT框架，将策略获取转化为程序合成过程，允许用户用自然语言纠正错误，并将修正蒸馏为持久技能库。在Franka Panda机器人上，该方法在复杂长程任务（如衣物折叠）上优于VLA模型，且随技能库累积减少人工干预。

<h3 id="329">[329] Maximum Satisfiability of Simple Temporal Problems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23785](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23785) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Johannes K. Fichte, Johanna Groven, Peter Jonsson, Victor Lagerkvist, Jorke M. de Vlas
- **Subjects**: cs.CC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Temporal Reasoning, Complexity Theory, Constraint Reasoning
- **Summary**: 简单时间问题STP的最大一致子集求解是NP难的。本文分析参数复杂性，证明以变量数n为参数时W[1]-难，并给出O*(k^n)算法和基于树宽的XP算法。与其他CSP对比表明，MAXSTP通常更难，但通过组合参数可实现FPT。

<h3 id="330">[330] PathScale-R1: Cross-scale Reasoning for Pathological Image Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23794](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23794) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chi Phan, Tianyi Zhang, Yufeng Wu, Qiaochu Xue, Jiajie Zhang, Linghan Cai, Zeyu Liu, Sudong Wang, Yueming Jin, Dan Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/iMVR-PL/PathScale-R1)
- **Summary**: 现有病理VLM多局限于单尺度推理，无法融合多分辨率特征。本文构建PathScale-VQA基准和PathScale-R1模型，通过对抗文本筛选和结构控制干扰采样鼓励跨尺度证据使用，并结合难度驱动推理蒸馏与强化学习优化。实验表明PathScale-R1在跨尺度推理任务上达到最先进水平，并有效泛化到单尺度任务。

<h3 id="331">[331] How Context Attribution Handles What the Model Already Knows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23804](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23804) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quoc-Huy Trinh, Lin Zhu, Sebastian Szyller
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Attribution, Faithfulness
- **Summary**: 当输入上下文与模型预训练知识重叠时，现有上下文归因方法无法区分上下文贡献与权重贡献。本文提出包含四个新指标的评估协议和WMDP-Cyber++基准，实验证明多种归因方法均产生不可靠得分，且无法有效分离两类贡献。

<h3 id="332">[332] A Frozen 12B Beats Frontier Models on Verified Work: 100% Accuracy, 0 Tokens, Bit-Exact, Forever</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23806](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23806) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sietse Schelpe
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG; cs.PF
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Verification, Knowledge Base
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/corbenicai/galahad-bench)
- **Summary**: 传统LLM每次查询都需要重新生成，成本高且不确定。本文冻结模型并构建持久已验证解决方案存储器，一旦问题族被解决并通过独立验证，新实例零token精确复现。在跨架构测试中准确率100%，且精确寻址优于近似检索。验证重用速度极快，能耗低。

<h3 id="333">[333] Indic DiarBench: A Multilingual Joint Diarization and ASR Benchmark for Indian Languages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23808](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23808) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Deovrat Mehendale, Aditya Mehndiratta, Dhruv Rathi, Kaushal Bhogale, Mitesh M. Khapra
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Speaker Diarization, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 印度语言缺乏联合说话人分离和ASR的公开基准。本文构建Indic DiarBench，包含22种语言108小时多说话人音频，带有时间对齐的说话人标注转录。评估商业系统和多模态LLM建立基线，为包容性语音技术研究提供资源。

<h3 id="334">[334] Earnings25: A Comprehensive 500-Hour Speech Benchmark for Finance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23813](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23813) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Denglin Jiang, Haoran Zhou, Anshul Wadhawan, Brendan Fahy, Vinay Ramesh, David Weisberg, Dmitriy Derkachevskiy, Helen Sheehan, Srivas Prasad, Michele Franceschini
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Benchmark, Financial AI
- **Summary**: 金融领域缺乏大规模ASR基准。本文发布Earnings25，包含498小时标普500财报电话会议全长测试集和46小时分段集，提供结构化元数据及Whisper和Parakeet-TDT基线。支持说话人和行业感知的WER评估。

<h3 id="335">[335] Kalypso: Relational LLM Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23815](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23815) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hojae Son, Md Ashraful Islam, Huy Gia Cao, Hui Guan, Marco Serafini
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, Query Processing, KV Cache
- **Summary**: 现有LLM服务系统不感知查询计划，导致KV缓存无法有效复用。本文提出关系型LLM服务Kalypso，通过自适应内存感知调度实现流水线算子间KV缓存重用。实验表明，Kalypso将查询完成时间加速最高4.57倍。

<h3 id="336">[336] TriShieldRAG: A Three-Ring Defense-in-Depth Framework Against Knowledge Corruption in Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23838](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23838) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Susil Kumar Mohanty, Rohit Patel, Kosuru Yuvaraj, Jeenal Chaudhary, Disha Singhania
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Security, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: RAG系统容易受知识投毒攻击，小量恶意文档即可操纵答案。本文提出TriShieldRAG三层防御框架，包括文档摄入检查、检索重排序和跨模型共识，并引入一次重检索。在PoisonedRAG攻击下，攻击成功率从约91%降至约13%，同时保持良性查询准确率。

<h3 id="337">[337] Limbomorphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23842](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23842) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alex Alvarez, Michael Levin
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Emergent Behavior, Artificial Life
- **Venue**: ALIFE 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究了在无明确智能体、环境或交互规则的人工生命系统中，如何通过用户审美选择进化出类似生物的形态（Limbomorphs），并探讨其行为是否具有目标导向性。结果发现这些形态对输入扰动表现出物种特异性反应，但尚不确定是否反映真正的导航意图。

<h3 id="338">[338] A Coulomb Particle Model for Learning Kernel Attention in Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23869](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23869) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Masoud Badiei Khuzani, Sharath Honnaiah, Atiq Islam, Alex Cozzi, Abraham Bagherjeiran
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Kernel Attention
- **Summary**: 提出一种基于库仑粒子模型的方法，通过优化核-目标对齐并加入Riesz/库仑排斥势来学习随机特征分布，用于线性化Transformer的核注意力。实验表明该方法在保持线性推理复杂度的同时提高了准确率、校准度和鲁棒性。

<h3 id="339">[339] MulRobBench: A Decision-Level Benchmark for Safe and Security-Policy-Compliant Multimodal UAV Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23870](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23870) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Belal S. Alsinglawi, Weizheng Wang, Junyi Wu, Yi Jiang, Lianhai Lin, Merouane Debbah, Izzat Alsmadi
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, UAV Agent, Safety Evaluation
- **Summary**: 提出了MulRobBench，一个针对智慧城市环境中视觉-语言-动作（VLA）无人机智能体的协议条件基准，集成多模态观测、安全策略和动作级安全评估。发现最佳模型语义协议决策得分仅0.5141，并识别出模态信任选择、约束提取等主要决策不稳定因素。

<h3 id="340">[340] Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Predicting Nitrous Oxide Flux</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23880](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23880) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Freddy Yu, Jashanjeet Kaur Dhaliwal, Subhadeep Chakraborty
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Agricultural AI, Environmental AI
- **Summary**: 利用物理信息神经网络（PINN）预测农业一氧化二氮（N2O）通量，基于DayCent模型推导物理残差。实验表明物理约束在分布外验证中提升了鲁棒性，但跨站点泛化仍有挑战，负R²值表明模型在新站点上预测能力有限。

<h3 id="341">[341] Harnessing X-ray Absorption Spectroscopy Data through Multimodal Mining of Battery Literature</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23886](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23886) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tanjin He, Aikaterini Vriza, Logan Ward, Xu Huang, Yiming Chen, Anubhav Jain, Gerbrand Ceder, Rajeev S. Assary, Ian T. Foster, Maria K. Y. Chan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.DL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Materials Science, Multimodal Learning, Scientific Discovery
- **Summary**: 通过多模态（图像和文本）文献挖掘，从电池文献中提取X射线吸收光谱（XAS）数据，构建了包含13,740条光谱的开放性数据集。该数据集为大规模XAS分析、跨实验室比较和自主材料发现提供了基础。

<h3 id="342">[342] Visible to the Court: How AI Is (and Isn't) Litigated in U.S. Federal Court Opinions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23888](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23888) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Julie Yu, Rock Yuren Pang, Jevan Hutson, Katharina Reinecke
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Legal AI, AI Governance, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 系统审查了559份美国联邦法院意见，分类了AI争议主题、涉及技术及诉讼方，发现法院仅捕获了AI风险环境的部分。法院主要依赖现有法律原则而非制定新的AI法律，导致某些AI危害未得到解决。

<h3 id="343">[343] Embodied GPT-5.1: Evidence of a World Model?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23899](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23899) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Roberto Spinelli, Thiago C. Martins
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, World Model, Robotics
- **Venue**: CROS 2026
- **Summary**: 探索性研究显示，大型多模态语言模型GPT-5.1在无实体训练的情况下，能作为物理移动机器人的高层控制器，展现出空间推理和物理理解迹象。该结果挑战了传统认知中物理身体是智能必要前提的观点。

<h3 id="344">[344] Understanding Tone-Dependent Inference Cost in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23915](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23915) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Akhil Kumar, Om Dobariya
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Prompt Engineering, Cost Optimization
- **Summary**: 研究提示语气如何影响LLM的准确性和推理成本（输出令牌消耗），发现输出令牌长度变化（高达44.3%）远大于准确率变化。不同语气在帕累托前沿上表现不同，影响计费资源消耗，提示语气不仅影响答案质量也影响推理成本。

<h3 id="345">[345] DuoAD: Leveraging [CLS] Dual Characteristics for Training-Free Few-Shot Anomaly Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23924](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23924) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jyun-Ze Tang, Po-Han Huang, Ming-Ching Chang, Chih-Fan Hsu, Jeng-Lin Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Vision Transformer, Few-Shot Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/inventec-ai-center/DuoAD)
- **Summary**: 利用ViT [CLS]令牌的双重特性（全局语义表示和注意力图异常区域），提出无需训练的参数自适应的异常检测框架。在单样本设置下，在MVTec-AD等数据集上达到SOTA，Image-AUC分别为97.7%、93.2%和84.5%。

<h3 id="346">[346] SpecBox: Speculative Sandbox Scheduling for Efficient LLM Agent Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23933](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23933) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yihui Zhang, Tianyu Wo, Jinghao Wang, Xiaoyang Sun, Menghao Zhang, Cangzhou Yuan, Li Li, Chunming Hu, Albert Y. Zomaya, Renyu Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.PF
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, LLM Agent, Speculative Execution
- **Summary**: 提出SpecBox，一种针对LLM智能体执行的运行时，通过推测性沙箱预分配、语义缓存和共享内存传输等优化。实验表明，该框架将P99端到端延迟降低2.9倍，峰值内存消耗降低45.9%。

<h3 id="347">[347] Understanding Machine Unlearning Through the Lens of Mode Connectivity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23970](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23970) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiali Cheng, Hadi Amiri
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Loss Landscape, Privacy
- **Summary**: 从模式连接角度研究机器遗忘，发现遗忘模型通常位于连通盆地中，且线性连接表明近似遗忘方法在机制上不同于重新训练。基于模式连接的集成可以提高泛化性和鲁棒性。

<h3 id="348">[348] Tag Questions and the Generational Reversal of Sycophancy Across 45 Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23976](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23976) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tapan Parikh
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sycophancy, LLM Evaluation, Prompt Engineering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/tap2k/modelun)
- **Summary**: 测量45个语言模型中确认性附加问句对模型回答的影响，发现效应从+32%到-32%，且模型家族内随代际推进从迎合转向抵抗。研究表明模型对表面句法结构的反应而非原则性立场，且语气极性比存在更重要。

<h3 id="349">[349] Multimodal Semantic-Probabilistic Objectness for Open World Object Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23981](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23981) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weijun Tian, Rui Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 提出MSPO，一个轻量级语义校准框架，通过已知类别的语言先验来增强概率目标性，用于开放世界目标检测。实验在M-OWODB和S-OWODB上提升性能，同时保持竞争性的未知召回率，PASCAL VOC最终mAP提升2.7点。

<h3 id="350">[350] Moral Hazard in Multi-Agent Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23982](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23982) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dane Malenfant
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Game Theory
- **Venue**: COLM 2026
- **Summary**: 引入对话道德风险博弈，操作化隐藏行动结构，评估7个开源语言模型的行为。发现基础模型常保留局部奖励而不实现团队成功，且优化可能改变总体回报但未恢复预期合作机制。

<h3 id="351">[351] Adaptive Data Admission and Retention for Streaming Federated Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23987](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23987) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhuoyi Zhao, Ben Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Online Learning, Resource Allocation
- **Summary**: 研究具有有限客户端内存的流式联邦学习，提出自适应数据准入和保留策略（ACDPP），在采样成本预算和缓冲区约束下最小化累积超额群体风险。实验证明该策略接近oracle基准且满足约束。

<h3 id="352">[352] SyRuP: Enhancing System-Prompt Following via Reward-Guided Prediction in LLM Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.23991](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23991) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seoyeon Kim, Minjae Kang, Jaehyung Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Prompt Engineering, LLM Alignment, Decoding Strategy
- **Summary**: 提出SyRuP，一种解码时框架，通过交叉注意力奖励头从系统提示条件偏好对中学习令牌级遵循分数，在推理时重排候选。实验表明该系统在提示遵循基准上持续优于基线，且计算开销适中。

<h3 id="353">[353] Agentic Cloud Decoys: A Deception-Driven Framework for Autonomous Intrusion Investigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24006](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24006) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohan Manivannan, Dalal Alharthi
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Cybersecurity, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 针对云环境中海量遥测数据导致入侵分析困难的问题，提出基于自主语言模型代理的Cloud Decoy AI Agent框架，通过会话聚合和动态提示生成缩小从可疑活动到分析报告的距离。在10个AWS S3场景中，9个完全重构，无不可追溯断言，延迟4-5分钟。

<h3 id="354">[354] Disentangling Semantic Attention from Structural Bias in the Attention Manifold</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24017](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24017) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pengkun Jiao, Bin Zhu, Jingjing Chen, Yu-gang Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, LLM Hallucination, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大语言模型中视觉注意力沉没导致的结构性视觉信号稀释问题，提出无需训练的SPAR干预方法，通过纯化结构噪声并重新分配注意力预算到最有信息量的视觉区域。在幻觉基准上有效恢复视觉基础，计算开销极小。

<h3 id="355">[355] HELIOS: An LLM-Driven Autonomous Indirect Trajectory Optimization Agent</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24051](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24051) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: An-yi Huang
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.IM; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, Scientific Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对低推力轨迹优化中庞特里亚金最小值原理推导繁琐、代码重复和初值敏感问题，提出基于大语言模型的自主优化代理HELIOS，自动进行符号推导、代码生成和数值求解。在11个测试场景中全部正确求解，最佳编译成功率100%，模型规模与推导能力正相关。

<h3 id="356">[356] Capacity-Aware Deep Learning for Generalizable Traffic Volume Estimation Across Links and Cities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24056](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24056) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Léo Hein, Giovanni De Nunzio, Aurélie Pirayre, Laurent Najman
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Traffic Forecasting, Out-of-Distribution Detection
- **Summary**: 针对交通流量估计依赖传感器密度难以推广到稀疏网络的问题，提出容量感知公式，将流量建模为结构容量与利用率的乘积，嵌入交通理论约束。在空间分布漂移下一致优于基线。

<h3 id="357">[357] ACRL: Adaptive Control of Training-Inference Discrepancy for Stable Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24062](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24062) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenwu Fan, Qihong Lin, Zhijie Xia, Zhuo Zheng, Sihao Wang, Qiang Chen, Liangsheng Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Reinforcement Learning, Quantization
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型强化学习训练中训练-推理不一致导致的不稳定性问题，提出自适应控制RL（ACRL），通过自适应维持差异在合理范围内稳定训练。在FP8量化下，ACRL保持稳定并达到BF16精度，优于重要性采样方法。

<h3 id="358">[358] MarineEVT: Advancing Event-Centric Marine Video Understanding via Visual Tool Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24064](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24064) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tuan-An To, Yuk-Kwan Wong, Tuan-Anh Vu, Ziqiang Zheng, Sai-Kit Yeung
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Vision-Language Model, Tool Learning
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 针对海洋视频理解中领域专业性强和事件稀疏问题，构建首个事件级海洋视频理解数据集MarineEVT，并提出事件级视觉工具集成推理EVT-R1。EVT-R1在多项指标上分别超越顶级开源和商业模型5.22和11.09分。

<h3 id="359">[359] Towards simultaneous decoding of kinetic and kinematic movement parameters during grasp and lift task by noninvasive brain imaging</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24081](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24081) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Parth G. Dangi, Yogesh Kumaar Meena
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.ET
- **Tags**: Brain-Computer Interface, EEG Classification
- **Venue**: IEEE SMC 2026
- **Summary**: 针对脑机接口中同时解码运动学与动力学参数困难的问题，提出三种回归模型（偏最小二乘、多层感知机、注意力模型）从EEG信号解码多参数。注意力模型取得R²=0.8，延迟29.2毫秒。

<h3 id="360">[360] LU-500: A Logo Benchmark for Concept Unlearning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24101](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24101) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Keyu Li, Jin Gao, Jialing Zhang, Dequan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Concept Erasure, Text-to-Image, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对概念遗忘评估忽视局部化目标（如商标）的问题，构建LU-500基准（含显式和隐式轨道），并提出多粒度评估协议。现有方法难以在不改变非目标内容的情况下移除商标，提示过滤无法替代权重级解耦。

<h3 id="361">[361] A Case Study on the Acceptance of a Humanoid Robotic Head Employed in Three Public Spaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24113](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24113) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marcel Heisler, Luca Randecker, Christian Becker-Asano
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Human-Robot Interaction, Usability Evaluation
- **Venue**: RO-MAN 2026
- **Summary**: 针对人形机器人接受度依赖场景的问题，将具有自然语言处理和情感模拟功能的机器人部署于三个公共场所。问卷调查显示用户认为机器人有用且易用，但旅游信息和图书馆比办公室更合适，响应速度需改进。

<h3 id="362">[362] EEGForceFusion: Joint Tokenised-Continuous Representation Learning for Subject-Independent Grasp Force Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24126](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24126) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sankalp Sunil Turankar, Yogesh Kumar Meena
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.ET
- **Tags**: Brain-Computer Interface, EEG Classification, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: IEEE SMC 2026
- **Summary**: 针对脑机接口中连续抓力解码跨被试泛化难的问题，提出混合框架结合卷积-循环学习、量化分词和Transformer建模连续与分词化表示。在留一被试条件下R²=0.817离线，0.793模拟实时，延迟适合实时部署。

<h3 id="363">[363] Monitoring Post-Disaster Urban Recovery Using High-Resolution SAR Time Series and Unsupervised Learning: Evidence from the 2023 Türkiye-Syria Earthquake</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24180](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24180) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luigi Russo, Deodato Tapete, Silvia Liberata Ullo, Paolo Gamba
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Anomaly Detection, Disaster Response
- **Summary**: 针对灾后恢复监测缺乏地面真值的问题，提出基于多时相SAR和深度学习异常检测的无监督框架。在2023土耳其-叙利亚地震四城市应用，揭示重建动态，SAR异常比夜间灯光更早捕捉结构变化。

<h3 id="364">[364] Not Forgotten: Implementation and Evaluation of a Personalized Episodic Memory for the Humanoid Robot Head Kim</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24190](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24190) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Steve Aschenbrenner, Marcel Heisler, Thomas Sievers, Christian Becker-Asano
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Human-Robot Interaction, Memory Architecture, Dialogue System
- **Summary**: 针对社交机器人缺乏跨会话记忆的问题，为机器人Kim实现轻量级情景记忆模块，结合向量检索和混合评分函数注入对话。用户研究（N=43）显示感知社交性显著提升（d=0.60），可信度和温暖感增强，无隐私不适。

<h3 id="365">[365] StanceFlip: A Comprehensive Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Multimodal Conversational Stance Flipping Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24191](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24191) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Heyan Chai, Xin Li, Wenjie Wang, Jianyang Qin, Chaoyang Li, Lu Wang, Hao Chen, Qing Liao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Stance Detection, Multimodal Learning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对多模态对话立场检测动态演变和模态线索利用不足的问题，提出StanceFlip基准（含立场六元组提取和立场反转归因子任务）及ConStaFF框架（内含Thought-of-Stance推理）。在立场提取和反转归因上超越多模态大语言模型基线。

<h3 id="366">[366] Every Client Is an Environment: Federated De-confounding for Spatio-Temporal Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24218](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24218) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qingxiang Liu, Anqi Liang, Heng Wang, Yuxuan Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Time Series Forecasting, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 针对联邦时空预测中客户异质性被简化为优化挑战的问题，提出将客户视为因果环境的联邦去混淆框架，学习全局原型码本。理论推导去混淆界，实验一致优于联邦基线，提供可迁移的环境表示。

<h3 id="367">[367] FilmBench: A Film-Grade Benchmark for Cinematic Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24241](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24241) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shengyi Wang, Niantong Li, Guangzheng Hu, Hong Qi, Fei Ding, Weixu Qiao, Jinlin Wang, Xiaotong Lv, Peng Han, Zimeng Li, Fanshu Ding, Yushu Wang, Han Wu, Jingjing Chen, Chongxiao Wang, Yanhao Wu, Chenglong Huang, Xiaoqian Zhu, Jie Tian, Hua Li, Jingjing Fan, Mingshuang Tang, Zhong Li, Hengxia Qiang, Weibin Chen, Jinyang Zhen, Bing Zhao, Lin Qu, Jing Li, Hu Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Benchmark, Video Quality Assessment
- **Summary**: 针对现有视频生成基准使用网络提示和粗粒度评价、缺乏专业电影标准的问题，构建FilmBench（基于获奖电影片段提示，三级电影学术评价分类法），并开发专家级自动评价代理。评价器与人类排序的Spearman ρ=0.95，分数远低于网络基准。

<h3 id="368">[368] ML-based Predictive Models for Power Consumption in Virtualised O-RANs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24256](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24256) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rishu Raj, Genevieve Akude, Urooj Tariq, Daniel Kilper
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Energy Efficiency, Time Series Forecasting, Wireless Networks
- **Summary**: 针对虚拟化O-RAN中传统功率模型不足的问题，采用特征提取和回归模型（DNN、正则化DNN、混合DNN-XGBoost）预测功耗。混合模型均方相对误差低于0.5%。

<h3 id="369">[369] Physics-Guided Generative AI for Property-Targeted 3D Porous Media Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24274](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24274) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Peng Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Inverse Design
- **Summary**: 针对三维多孔介质逆设计中小结构变化显著影响输运行为的挑战，提出一个物理引导的生成式AI框架，结合属性感知变分自编码器、条件潜空间扩散模型和可微结构-属性代理模型，实现目标孔隙率和方向渗透率控制。实验表明，在程序生成和真实微CT数据集上，该方法在目标属性匹配、方向渗透率控制和属性相关性方面优于现有基线。

<h3 id="370">[370] A Computational Ethical Framework for Financial Digital Phenotyping for Mental Health</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24275](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24275) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Oluwadara Adedeji, Michael Mayowa Farayola, Jeff Brozena, Irina Tal, Regina Connolly, Mark Matthews
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Formal Methods, Mental Health
- **Venue**: CIBB 2026
- **Summary**: 解决数字表型系统中伦理要求与系统级验证之间的鸿沟，提出计算伦理框架，将伦理需求形式化为道义时序逻辑约束，并通过概念性伦理代理监督系统，使用Z3 SMT求解器验证。结果表明框架在逻辑上一致，能够通过反例排除伦理属性违反，实现连续可验证的伦理检查。

<h3 id="371">[371] The Tokenizer Tax: Quantifying and Explaining the Cross-Lingual Cost of Subword Tokenization for Indian Languages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24276](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24276) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Priyansh Srivastava
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Tokenization, Multilingual Learning, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 量化子词分词器对印度语言的跨语言成本，使用FLORES-200语料库测量6种分词器和14种语言的标记化生育率。印度语言平均面临8倍标记化税（马拉雅拉姆语达13倍），主要由字节对合并失败导致（r=0.89），多语言分词器可将税降低73%。

<h3 id="372">[372] Teacher Knows It Best: Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking and Tipping Points in Networked Langevin Dynamics AI Sycophancy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24304](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24304) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sayantari Ghosh, Saumik Bhattacharya, Partha Pratim Chakrabarti
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; math.DS
- **Tags**: Sycophancy, Social Network Analysis, Dynamical Systems
- **Summary**: 针对AI谄媚现象导致社会网络中错误信念持续强化的问题，构建统计物理模型，通过度加权平均场近似导出临界倾覆时间的解析解。验证了拓扑干预策略的普适尺度行为，并在预算约束下证明集中快速干预优于分散慢速干预。

<h3 id="373">[373] Beyond Aggregate Risk: Role-Stratified Conformal Risk Control for LLM Tool Calls</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24343](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24343) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md Ashikur Rahman, Md Arifur Rahman, Niamul Hassan Samin, Khandaker Rifah Tasnia, Sifat Rahman Ahona, Juena Ahmed Noshin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Conformal Prediction, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 针对现有共形风险控制在整个动作层面掩盖高风险字段失败的问题，提出角色分层的逐字段共形风险控制，为语义参数角色设置独立阈值和风险预算。在AgentDojo和InjecAgent上使用六种语言模型，该方法实现了一致的角色特定预算遵从性，优于聚合级别的认证。

<h3 id="374">[374] DeepFaith: Evidence-Grounded LLMs for Faithful Incident Reporting in Multi-Stage APT Defense</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24348](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24348) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Trung V. Phan, Tri Gia Nguyen, Thomas Bauschert
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Faithfulness, Report Generation
- **Summary**: 解决LLM在APT防御报告中产生幻觉或缺乏依据的问题，提出DeepFaith框架，通过统一证据表示、证据引导提示和忠实性感知生成，将自主防御模块输出转化为与系统证据对齐的自然语言报告。实验将忠实性从0.68提升至0.92，无依据声明从0.32降至0.08。

<h3 id="375">[375] Closed-Loop Validation-Repair for Healthcare Interoperability: A Multi-Model Study of Schema Compliance in Clinical LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24371](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24371) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jianru Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Evaluation, Clinical NLP
- **Venue**: SMC 2026
- **Summary**: 针对临床LLM输出不符合医疗模式（如ICD-10、HL7 FHIR）的问题，评估三种开源模型，采用闭环验证-修复框架。基线合规率85.9-91.6%，修复后达到99.0%；96%的失败为格式违规；统计显著（p\<0.001）。

<h3 id="376">[376] MXAttention: Data-Free Optimal Scaling and Pre-Normalization Quantization for MXFP4 Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24377](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24377) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jianlin Yu, Jing Lin, Linghui Kong, Aiyue Chen, Weiyi Sun, Chenyu Zeng, Wangli Lan, Jinxi Li, Zhuo Zheng, Ziyang Yue, Danning Ke, Fei Yi, Tianchi Hu, Yuan Ding, Yiwu Yao, Junsong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Quantization, Diffusion Model, Video Generation
- **Summary**: 针对MXFP4注意力量化导致扩散视频生成质量下降的问题，提出MXAttention框架，包含通用最优缩放（UOS）和预归一化量化（PNQ）。在Wan2.2和HunyuanVideo上，UOS+PNQ弥合了OCP MXFP4与FP16之间至少95%的VBench成像质量差距，且质量损失低于0.01。

<h3 id="377">[377] Regulating for AI Legitimacy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24391](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24391) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gilad Abiri
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, AI Policy, AI Governance
- **Summary**: 论证AI治理中合法性（legitimacy）作为独立规制目标的必要性，区别于对齐（alignment）。指出透明性、私人权力和行政自动化三个合法性危机点，提出薄合法性与厚合法性概念，并给出整合、熟悉性和可争议性三项原则。

<h3 id="378">[378] The SpiNNaker2 chip: a many-core platform for flexible and scalable brain-inspired computing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24396](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24396) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stefan Scholze, Johannes Partzsch, Sebastian Höppner, Florian Kelber, Andreas Dixius, Marco Stolba, Sirine Arfa, Marc Berthel, Georg Ellguth, Jim Garside, Hector A. Gonzalez, Stephan Hartmann, Thomas Kiel-Hocker, Dongwei Hu, Matthias Jobst, Khaleelulla Khan Nazeer, Tim Langer, Chen Liu, Gengting Liu, Matthias Lohrmann, Mantas Mikaitis, Felix Neumärker, Amirhossein Rostami, Stefan Schiefer, Tilo Schubert, Delong Shang, Bernhard Vogginger, Yexin Yan, Steve Furber, Christian Mayr
- **Subjects**: cs.ET; cs.AI; cs.AR; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Neuromorphic Computing, Hardware Architecture, Energy Efficiency
- **Summary**: 为解决深度学习和神经形态计算的高效硬件需求，介绍SpiNNaker2芯片，包含152个ARM M4F处理元素和专用加速器，支持事件驱动通信。在INT8工作负载下可达4.5 TOPS，能效2.7 TOPS/W，支持超过15万神经元和18亿突触事件/秒。

<h3 id="379">[379] Multivariate Time Series Forecasting with Adaptive Non-Local Observables</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24399](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24399) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yu-Ting Lee, Huan-Hsin Tseng, Samuel Yen-Chi Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Quantum Machine Learning
- **Summary**: 针对量子神经网络用于时间序列预测时固定局部测量限制表达力的问题，提出MTSF-ANO混合模型，集成变分量子电路和自适应非局域可观测量。在ETT数据集上，20个设定中17个的MSE排名前二，在ETTh1上比最强基线提升最多20%。

<h3 id="380">[380] DraftExpert: Expansion-Aware Self-Speculative Decoding for End-Device MoE Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24434](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24434) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dengke Han
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Speculative Decoding, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Summary**: 针对MoE模型在端设备上自推测解码中专家加载瓶颈和低接受率问题，提出DraftExpert框架，通过自蒸馏训练轻量级草稿专家，结合置信度扩展截断和目标专家预取。在DeepSeek-V2-Lite和Moonlight-16B-A3B上，解码吞吐量提升1.45倍，草稿接受率84-87%。

<h3 id="381">[381] LEX-EC: A Lexical Evidence-Channel Audit Framework for Zero-Shot LLM Personality Classification in Black-Box Settings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24435](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24435) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Brittany Harbison, Ashok K. Goel
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Interpretability, Personality Recognition
- **Summary**: 针对LLM人格分类可解释性不足的问题，提出LEX-EC黑盒审计框架，结合流行度与一致性诊断及可控词汇消融，区分边际分布效应与特质关联信号。实验揭示不同文本类型的人格关联差异，并验证词汇消融和提示敏感性。

<h3 id="382">[382] Evaluating RAG for French immigration law: a benchmark and baseline study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24449](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24449) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Annia Abtout, Julien Delaunay, Monika Ewa Rakoczy
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Legal AI, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对法国移民法领域缺乏AI基准的问题，构建公开基准并评估稠密检索增强（RAG）的效果。使用Qwen3.5-9B和27B模型在52个合成档案上比较，发现检索增强可以改善行政指导，尤其在许可类型准确率上表现显著。

<h3 id="383">[383] ESRVS: Extreme Semi-Supervised Retinal Vessel Segmentation with a Single Annotated Image</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24453](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24453) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingzhi Xu, Yizhe Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical Image Segmentation, Semi-Supervised Learning, Foundation Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/IAANNH/ESRVS)
- **Summary**: 针对医学图像分割中标注成本高的问题，提出极端半监督视网膜血管分割方法ESRVS，仅使用一张标注图像。通过DINOv3特征传递血管线索，构建多粒度血管原型，生成伪标签并精炼。在8个数据集上，6个Dice和clDice最优，所有8个HD95最优，保留93.7%的全监督Dice性能。

<h3 id="384">[384] UNIFUSION: Adapting Autoregressive Language Models into Discrete Diffusion under a Unified Reverse-Rate Objective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24507](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24507) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaoyi Jiang, Jingyuan Li, Yixuan Jiang, Wei Liu, Yi Zhu, Zuoqiang Shi, Pipi Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Language Model, Autoregressive Model, Pre-training
- **Summary**: 针对将预训练自回归语言模型适配到均匀噪声离散扩散的挑战，提出UNIFUSION方法，基于统一逆向速率目标进行持续预训练。在256步采样时，UNIFUSION在生成困惑度和熵的权衡上最优，并在WinoGrande、SIQA和BBH上达到扩散模型中最高准确率。

<h3 id="385">[385] DecoupleMix: Decoupled Ratio Search and Convex Allocation for Scalable VLM Data Recipes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24516](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24516) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiahao Xie, Zhongbin Guo, Qianle Wang, Ruiqi Lu, Dongling Xiao, Wanxuan Sun, Cheng Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Mixing, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型(VLM)预训练数据混合依赖启发式方法的问题，提出DecoupleMix框架，将数据构建分解为跨类别比率搜索和类别内凸优化，实现可复现的工程化数据配比。实验表明该方法优于启发式基线，且在小规模代理上发现的最优比率可无缝迁移至大规模训练，使用80B多模态继续预训练 token 后，VLM性能与使用更大多模态预算的强开源模型相当。

<h3 id="386">[386] Stress-Testing EEG Foundation Models for Clinical Decoding: Dataset Identity and Targeted Negative Controls</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24519](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24519) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marzieh Zare
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: EEG Foundation Model, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对EEG基础模型在临床解码中的跨人群迁移性和阴性对照鲁棒性不明的问题，系统评估了6个模型在5项临床任务上的表现。发现结论强烈依赖于评估单元、数据集偏移和比较器强度，例如在韩国痴呆症数据集上，冻结REVE的AUROC为0.568，而经典特征达0.769；随机初始化的编码器甚至优于预训练REVE。最清晰的阳性结果为CHB-MIT癫痫检测，REVE比随机初始化高9.2个百分点。

<h3 id="387">[387] EchoBridge: Long-Tail-Aware ECG-Echocardiography Text Alignment for Echocardiography-Derived Cardiac Findings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24553](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24553) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaocheng Fang, Jieyi Cai, Guangkun Nie, Haoyu Wang, Jiarui Jin, Yujie Xiao, Bo Liu, Chenyang He, Qinghao Zhao, Gaofeng Cheng, Hongyan Li, Shenda Hong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multimodal Learning, Long-Tailed Learning
- **Summary**: 针对全局心电-超声文本对齐会纠缠模态特定因素且长尾分布导致低患病条件监督稀疏的问题，提出EchoBridge，包含互补共享-私有投影(CSPP)和自适应原型边界校准(APBC)。在EchoNext-Mini和多中心数据集上，无分类器推理的AUROC、AUPRC和F1分别比最强基线提升7.88、5.61和4.54个百分点，且对多数病症（包括低患病率瓣膜病变）均有提升。

<h3 id="388">[388] LOCKS: Page-Local Compact Key Summaries for Efficient Long-Context Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24555](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24555) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junsung Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Inference, Sparse Attention
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文LLM服务中KV缓存读取瓶颈，提出LOCKS：为每个页面提供其光谱摘要（约缓存大小的1/10），通过log-sum-exp估计注意力质量并只选择top页面。在LongBench-v1上匹配全缓存质量，在100K+上下文中仅关注约2%的token，解码延迟减半（1M token时加速2.0倍），可直接作为vLLM插件使用。

<h3 id="389">[389] BettiSplit: Topology-Guided Privacy-Aware Split Learning Against Feature Inversion and Gradient Leakage</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24556](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24556) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Akarsh K.Nair, Muhammad Arifur Rahman, David Brown, Mufti Mahmud
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Privacy, Split Learning, Topological Data Analysis
- **Summary**: 针对分割学习中分割点选择不当导致特征反转和梯度泄露的隐私风险，提出基于持续Betti复杂度的拓扑引导框架BettiSafe，无需执行攻击即可识别隐私敏感层。相比基于深度的启发式方法，对特征反转的抵抗性提升2-5倍，同时保持分类精度；基于Betti的正则化使反转难度提升近5倍。

<h3 id="390">[390] EgoPlay: Event-Triggered Video Editing for Egocentric Streams</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24560](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24560) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinjie Mai, Gordon Guocheng Qian, Willi Menapace, Arpit Sahni, Chaoyang Wang, Ashkan Mirzaei, Runjia Li, Sergey Tulyakov, Bernard Ghanem, Peter Wonka, Rameen Abdal
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Editing, Diffusion Model
- **Venue**: SIGGRAPH Asia 2026
- **Summary**: 针对第一人称视频的触发式编辑（如“当X发生时，执行Y”），提出EgoPlay，微调预训练V2V扩散Transformer，端到端学习事件识别、时间约束和像素编辑。在Ego4D基准上，编辑质量、视觉质量和背景一致性相对最强基线EgoEdit提升17.7%、16.9%和16.4%，且GPU内存减半。

<h3 id="391">[391] The Visual Bottleneck: Sparse-Frame Adaptation of MLLMs for Joint Spatial-Temporal Video Grounding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24570](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24570) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiameng Zhang, Srikanth Madikeri
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Grounding, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 针对MLLM在稀疏帧输入下时空视频定位性能严重下降（Qwen3-VL从56.0%降至22.3% temporal mIoU）的问题，系统研究了训练策略。发现视觉特征提取是主要瓶颈，仅微调最后三层ViT（4%参数）即可达到68.8% temporal mIoU，超过零样本8B模型密集输入12.8个百分点；边界感知采样Hybrid16进一步比均匀采样提升26点。

<h3 id="392">[392] CADER: Confidence-Aware Dynamic Evidence Reasoning for Long-Video Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24582](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24582) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinlong Yang, Wenhao Zhang, Kuanwei Lin, Sijie Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Tool Learning, Confidence Calibration
- **Summary**: 针对长视频推理中统一推理策略无法适应不同难度的问题，提出CADER（置信度感知动态证据推理），无训练框架。先对所有均匀采样帧进行全局推理，基于logit-margin置信度让高置信样本提前退出；不确定样本则激活第二阶段的工具增强循环，结合时间裁剪、语义验证和相关性引导重采样定位证据。在多个VideoQA基准上提升长视频推理性能。

<h3 id="393">[393] D-Score: A Spectral Hidden-State Signal for Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24586](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24586) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bianca Raimondi, Davide Evangelista, Maurizio Gabbrielli, Elena Loli Piccolomini
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, LLM Evaluation, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对LLM生成流畅但虚假文本的幻觉检测问题，从隐藏激活几何出发提出D-Score，仅需单次前向计算隐藏激活矩阵奇异值分布中接近最大奇异值的个数。在FAVA-Annotation和RAGTruth上，D-Score无需外部验证器、检索或多次生成，即表现出强幻觉检测能力。

<h3 id="394">[394] Evaluating the Impact of Explainable AI on Trust in AI-Assisted Code Review</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24601](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24601) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenhan Gao, Marvin Muñoz Barón, Umm-e Habiba, Daniel Graziotin, Stefan Wagner
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Code Review, Explainable AI, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Venue**: ISSTA 2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM自动代码审查中开发者难以评估有效性进而影响信任的问题，通过34人用户研究比较三种解释级别。完全解释条件感知信任最高（M=3.99/5），但中度解释条件获得最高赞同率（89.22%），无解释条件信任和赞同率均最低。解释级别未显著影响审查时间。

<h3 id="395">[395] Looping Is Not Reliability: State-Bound Evidence and Typed Revision Contracts for Agentic Code Repair</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24604](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24604) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xueping Gao, Jianwei Yang, Qiang Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Program Repair, Agentic Software
- **Summary**: 针对编码智能体生成-测试-修订循环中重复不能保证可靠性的问题，通过密封五种子研究（900条三修订轨迹）发现强制修订下当前正确率从0.820降至0.673。提出基于证据的带类型循环契约，将机制可强制执行子集实例化，绑定验证器证据到确切代码状态，保存已验证检查点并发出可审计接收凭证。

<h3 id="396">[396] Agentic Permissions Policy Algebra for Taint Confinement in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24625](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24625) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arseny Kravchenko, Vadim Liventsev, Innokentii Konstantinov, Ildar Iskhakov, Matvey Kukuy
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Information Flow Control
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体处理混合敏感数据时面临提示注入和推理错误导致的严重安全风险，提出APPA（智能体权限策略代数），一种信息流控制框架。通过引擎管理的上下文分支和预期获取强制，在获取数据前评估标签下降和缺失前提，生成行动补救计划；分支机制防止主要上下文被污染。在四模型多轮工具链基准上，将渗漏成功率从31%-50%降至0%-7%。

<h3 id="397">[397] Sparse Autoencoders Encode Both Concepts and Functions: The Downstream Geometry of Feature Effects</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24645](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24645) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Phu Gia Hoang, Anwoy Chatterjee, Tanmoy Chakraborty, Iryna Gurevych, Subhabrata Dutta
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Sparse Autoencoder, Mechanistic Interpretability, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对稀疏自编码器特征与模型行为之间联系不一致（特征可解释但因果效应弱或方向相反）的问题，引入特征效应几何分析(FEGA)，无监督分析移除特征后logit变化的空间几何。发现少数特征表现为可复用的方向，多数特征效应分散；区分了与静态信息相关的值类特征和与上下文操作相关的指针类特征，后者效应通常弥散。

<h3 id="398">[398] A corrective agentic hybrid RAG and an operations-grounded evaluation for a scientific facility</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24663](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24663) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rajat Sainju, Dariusz Jarosz, Hairong Shang, Michael Prince, Ryan M. Aydelott, Mathew J. Cherukara, Yine Sun, Michael D. Borland
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, Knowledge Graph, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 针对科学设施数十年运行知识分散、无统一检索的问题，提出APS-RAG（先进光子源检索增强生成），融合稠密、稀疏和知识图谱通道，加入修正智能体循环和ReAct执行器。构建APS-Bench（50问答对），全修正Agentic GraphRAG在严格核心信息召回上达70.3%，优于BM25基线（63.8%）。交叉编码重排序器贡献显著，移除后召回下降32.8%。

<h3 id="399">[399] Co-Learning for Missing Arbitrary Modalities in Multi-modal Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24683](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24683) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Francisco Mena, Dino Ienco, Roberto Interdonato, Cassio F. Dantas, Simon Besnard
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Missing Modality, Co-Learning
- **Venue**: Discovery Science 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/fmenat/Co4Miss)
- **Summary**: 针对多模态分类中训练和推理时模态可用性不一致（任意模态缺失）的问题，采用多模态协同学习框架而非融合，提出两种特征级和决策级方法。实验表明方法一在单模态缺失时更鲁棒，方法二在极端缺失（仅剩一个模态）时表现更好。

<h3 id="400">[400] Denial of Deadline: Network-Driven Accuracy Collapse in Distributed Inference Pipelines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24692](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24692) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jhonatan Tavori, Gur-Eyal Sela, Ion Stoica, Gil Zussman
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Adversarial Robustness, Distributed Inference
- **Summary**: 针对分布式推理管线中快速路径与慢速路径协调层暴露的新攻击面，展示形状化工作负载攻击（如Yo-Yo突发）可通过竞争慢速路径共享资源，使良性用户的慢速预测超过延迟截止线而被丢弃，导致准确率崩溃。在自动驾驶两层级边缘-云多目标跟踪管线中，约4000个突发请求使benign p99延迟从92ms增至2s，目标跟踪质量平均下降7.0 HOTA点，某些稀有类别（如停车标志）准确率损失近半。

<h3 id="401">[401] Efficient LLM-Generated Shuttling Compilers for Complex Trapped-Ion Architectures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24714](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24714) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fabian Kreppel, Reza Salkhordeh, Ferdinand Schmidt-Kaler, André Brinkmann
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.ET
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Trapped-Ion Quantum Computing
- **Summary**: 针对离子阱量子计算机中带编译器的开发耗时且依赖手动算法工程的问题，本文利用前沿大语言模型 Claude Opus 4.7 从书面规范自动生成并迭代精炼完整 Python 代码的搬运编译器，从线性分段陷阱扩展到复杂连接架构。实验表明，生成的编译器在搬运时间步数上最多减少 76%（线性陷阱）和 39%（结型陷阱），并将新架构的开发时间从数月缩短至数天。

<h3 id="402">[402] DataOrchestra: Learning to Orchestrate Per-Example Curation of Pretraining Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24717](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24717) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhen Huang, Yikun Wang, Shijie Xia, Pengfei Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Selection, Data Quality, Pre-training
- **Summary**: 现有预训练数据处理方法在语料或领域层面采用固定策略，缺乏对每个样本的自适应调整。本文提出 DataOrchestra 框架，为每个数据块动态编排处理流水线（丢弃、保留或清洗），并选择具体操作和生成指令。在 0.5B 至 7B 模型上的实验表明，该方法在 11 个基准上稳定优于单一处理方法，并能在数学继续预训练中减少计算量。

<h3 id="403">[403] The Physics of Multi-Turn Long-Horizon Planning: From Pre-training to Post-training via Single- and Multi-Teacher On-Policy Agentic Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24720](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24720) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianyi Men, Zhuoran Jin, Kang Liu, Jun Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Planning, Long-Horizon Planning
- **Summary**: 多轮长周期规划能力对基础模型智能体至关重要，但其获取、塑造和整合机制尚不明确。本文通过可控多轮环境系统研究预训练、后训练（GRPO 与 OPD）及多教师在线蒸馏（MOPD）阶段的规划能力，发现显式世界模型构建对泛化至关重要，次优轨迹会严重损害性能，且 MOPD 能通过收敛到共享规划模式整合能力。

<h3 id="404">[404] KANEx: Translating Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks' Interpretability to Medical Explainability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24730](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24730) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Krithi Shailya, Ananya Lakshmi Ravi, Venkatanathan K. V., Sowmya S. Sundaram, Gokul S. Krishnan, Aditi Anand, Balaraman Ravindran
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Kolmogorov-Arnold Network, Medical AI, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 医疗计算机视觉模型的黑箱特性削弱了临床信任，而视觉-语言模型生成的文本解释未解决底层视觉模型的不透明性。本文提出 KANEx 框架，利用 Kolmogorov-Arnold 网络（KAN）的符号可解释性来引导 VLM 推理，并设计 KAN-Map 热力图方法。在 MIMIC-CXR 数据集上，KAN 架构将视觉定位和下游推理质量提升 10%。

<h3 id="405">[405] Rethinking Classifier-Free Guidance in On-Policy Diffusion Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24731](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24731) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bingnan Li, Haozhe Wang, Haozhong Xiong, Fangtai Wu, Jinpeng Yu, Yang Shi, Jiaming Liu, Ruihua Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Knowledge Distillation, Classifier-Free Guidance
- **Summary**: 在扩散模型的在线蒸馏（OPD）中，分类器无关引导（CFG）下的速度匹配目标存在分支级欠确定问题，可能导致负分支不对称（NBA）。本文提出正向方向匹配（PDM），一种分支感知的 OPD 目标，分别约束正预测和 CFG 条件方向。在稠密到稀疏视频控制任务中，PDM 实现了更鲁棒有效的知识迁移。

<h3 id="406">[406] ClinFusion: A Vision-Centric Multimodal LLM System for Holistic Medical Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.24743](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24743) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hangjie Yuan, Yichen Qian, Zhiwei Tang, Xianzhe Xu, Lirong Wu, Sicheng Yang, Jinwang Wang, Pengju Wang, Zhitao Zeng, Yizeng Han, Yan Xing, Shengxuan Luo, Tao Feng, Qing Xie, Weigen Yao, Yi Yang, Zuozhu Liu, Jiasheng Tang, Shaocheng Wang, Jitao Wang, Jiahong Dong, Weihua Chen, Feng Xu, Fan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Medical AI, Medical Imaging
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/alibaba-damo-academy/ClinFusion;)
- **Summary**: 在临床中部署多模态大语言模型（MLLM）面临异构 2D/3D 医学图像处理及符合临床实践的评估挑战。本文提出 ClinFusion，采用级联空间感知局部融合编码器统一处理不同模态，并引入基于视觉的评估框架（MedIF-Bench 和 ROI 度量）。在 24 个基准上，ClinFusion 在 20 个上超越开源模型，在 13 个上超越 GPT-5.2 等专有模型，放射科医生盲评确认其报告质量最高。

<h3 id="408">[408] ANSR-DT: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Adaptive and Explainable Digital Twins</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG; cs.SC

<h3 id="409">[409] Robustness and Cybersecurity in the EU Artificial Intelligence Act</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.16184](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16184) (replaced)

<h3 id="410">[410] Hybrid AI-Physical Modeling for Penetration Bias Correction in X-band InSAR DEMs: A Greenland Case Study</h3>

<h3 id="411">[411] PD$^3$: A Project Duplication Detection Framework via Adapted Multi-Agent Debate</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.17492](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17492) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dezheng Bao, Yueci Yang, Chutian Yu, Xin Chen, Zeguo Fei, Xiang Yuan, Lijun Zhang, Jiangqian Huang, Zhengxuan Jiang, Daoze Zhang, Junru Chen, Yang Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Multi-Agent Debate
- **Summary**: 项目查重检测中现有方法依赖表面匹配或直接 LLM 判断，无法满足多对多参考集选择的实际需求。本文提出 PD³ 框架，采用局部多智能体辩论与全局循环调度的方式检索相关项目集，并生成量化查重分数。在 800 多个真实电力项目上，相关参考选择提升 4.05%，查重分数生成提升 9.77%，部署平台已节省 1344 万美元。

<h3 id="412">[412] LEGO Co-builder: Exploring Fine-Grained Vision-Language Modeling for Multimodal LEGO Assembly Assistants</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.05515](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05515) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haochen Huang, Yue Su, Xin Sun, Moonisa Ahsan, Mohammad Aliannejadi, Irene Viola, Zhaochun Ren, Chuang Yu, Aneta Lisowska, Artem Belopolsky, Koen Hindriks, Pablo Cesar, Junxiao Wang, Jiahuan Pei
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICMI 2026
- **Summary**: 视觉-语言模型在细粒度空间推理和物体状态检测上能力不足。本文构建了 LEGO Co-builder 基准，结合真实乐高组装逻辑与程序化生成的多模态场景，评估多个 VLM。结果显示物体检测准确率高（98.16%），但细粒度场景理解和状态检测仍有挑战（主题实体 F1 37.52%，状态检测 F1 40.54%）。

<h3 id="413">[413] Evo-DKD: Dual-Knowledge Decoding for Autonomous Ontology Evolution in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.21438](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21438) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vishal Raman, Vijai Aravindh R, Abhijith Ragav
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Ontology Evolution
- **Venue**: IEEE ICMLA 2026
- **Summary**: 本体和知识图谱需要持续演化但手动维护困难，LLM 难以保持结构化一致性。本文提出 Evo-DKD 双解码器框架，一个解码器生成本体编辑候选，另一个生成文本理由，通过动态门控机制协调。在医疗保健、语义搜索和文化遗产等用例上，精度和下游任务性能优于单一解码器基线。

<h3 id="414">[414] Rethinking Prospect Theory for LLMs: Revealing the Instability of Decision-Making under Epistemic Uncertainty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.08992](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08992) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rui Wang, Qihan Lin, Jiayu Liu, Qing Zong, Tianshi Zheng, Dadi Guo, Haochen Shi, Peixuan Han, Weiqi Wang, Yangqiu Song
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Decision Making, Prospect Theory
- **Summary**: 前景理论是否适用于描述 LLM 在语言不确定性下的决策行为尚不明确。本文基于行为经济学实验范式估计 LLM 的前景理论参数，并测试其在认知不确定性下的稳定性。研究发现前景理论无法一致描述 LLM 的决策行为，且对认知不确定性敏感，警告其在现实中的应用风险。

<h3 id="415">[415] TableMind: An Autonomous Programmatic Agent for Tool-Augmented Table Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.06278](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.06278) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chuang Jiang, Mingyue Cheng, Xiaoyu Tao, Qingyang Mao, Jie Ouyang, Qi Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Table Reasoning, LLM Agent, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: LLM 在表格推理中多采用单轮推理，存在上下文溢出、数值敏感性弱等问题。本文提出 TableMind，一个基于调优的自主程序化智能体，通过两阶段训练（SFT+RL）内化规划、动作和反思能力。在多个基准上一致优于先前方法，验证了训练自主智能体提升表格推理的有效性。

<h3 id="416">[416] Decentralized Causal Discovery using Judo Calculus</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.23942](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23942) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sridhar Mahadevan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Discovery, Decentralized Learning
- **Summary**: 因果效应依赖于具体情境，但传统因果发现方法未充分考虑此依赖。本文提出基于 judo 演算的分散式因果发现框架，利用 j-do-演算和层域理论形式化情境依赖的因果推断。在合成和真实数据集（生物学、经济学）上，该方法在计算效率和性能上优于经典方法。

<h3 id="417">[417] OS-Sentinel: Towards Safety-Enhanced Mobile GUI Agents via Hybrid Validation in Realistic Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.24411](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24411) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qiushi Sun, Mukai Li, Zhoumianze Liu, Zhihui Xie, Fangzhi Xu, Zhangyue Yin, Kanzhi Cheng, Zehao Li, Zichen Ding, Qi Liu, Zhiyong Wu, Zhuosheng Zhang, Ben Kao, Lingpeng Kong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV; cs.HC
- **Tags**: GUI Agent, Safety Evaluation, Agent Security
- **Summary**: 针对移动GUI代理的安全问题（系统入侵、隐私泄露）缺乏系统性检测的现状，提出了安全检测框架OS-Sentinel，结合形式化验证器（检测系统级违规）和VLM上下文裁判（评估情境风险），并在动态沙盒环境MobileRisk-Live上评估。实验表明，OS-Sentinel在多项指标上相比现有方法提升10%-30%。

<h3 id="418">[418] Multi-Modal Scene Graph with Kolmogorov-Arnold Experts for Audio-Visual Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.23304](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.23304) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zijian Fu, Changsheng Lv, Xianlin Zhang, Mengshi Qi, Huadong Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Audio-Visual Learning, Scene Graph, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/feel12348/SHRIKE)
- **Summary**: 现有音视频问答方法难以捕捉结构化信息和细粒度跨模态特征，为此首次引入多模态场景图（461,292个关系三元组）并设计基于KAN的混合专家模型以增强时间整合阶段的表达能力。在MUSIC-AVQA和MUSIC-AVQA v2基准上达到78.14%平均准确率，超越先前最佳方法QA-TIGER，并在v2所有配置中排名第一。

<h3 id="419">[419] Multi-agent DRL-based Lane Change Decision Model for Cooperative Platooning in Mixed Traffic</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.11809](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.11809) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zeyu Mu, Shangtong Zhang, B. Brian Park
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Motion Planning
- **Summary**: CAV初期部署阶段稀疏分布导致协同编队效果不佳，提出基于QMIX框架和CNN编码的混合多智能体换道决策模型（CNN-QMIX），并设计轨迹规划器和模型预测控制器确保换道安全。在不同CAV渗透率下训练评估，结果显示协同编队率最高提升26.2%，显著优于规则基线。

<h3 id="420">[420] Bayesian-LoRA: Probabilistic Low-Rank Adaptation of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21003](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21003) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Moule Lin, Shuhao Guan, Andrea Patane, David Gregg, Goetz Botterweck
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LoRA, Bayesian Inference, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 大语言模型在小数据集微调时校准性差，将LoRA重新形式化为基于稀疏高斯过程的概率低秩表示（Bayesian-LoRA），并证明LoRA是后验不确定性坍塌的极限情况。仅增加0.42M参数和约1.2倍训练成本，即可在30B模型上实现高达84%的ECE降低和76%的NLL降低，同时保持竞争性准确率。

<h3 id="421">[421] The Lattice Representation Hypothesis of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.01227](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01227) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bo Xiong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Interpretability, Logical Reasoning
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xiongbo010/lattice-representation-hypothesis)
- **Summary**: 为了理解LLM嵌入如何编码概念层次和逻辑操作，提出了格表示假说，将线性表示假说与形式概念分析统一，通过半空间交集诱导概念格。在WordNet子层次上的实验为LLM嵌入编码概念格及其逻辑结构提供了经验证据。

<h3 id="422">[422] PeopleSearchBench: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Evaluating AI-Powered People Search Platforms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.27476](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27476) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wei Wang, Tianyu Shi, Shuai Zhang, Boyang Xia, Zequn Xie, Chenyu Zeng, Qi Zhang, Lynn Ai, Yaqi Yu, Kaiming Zhang, Feiyue Tang, Lei Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Information Retrieval, AI Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对AI人搜索平台缺乏公认评估基准的问题，构建了PeopleSearchBench开源基准，包含119个真实查询和四个用例，采用标准事实验证管道生成二元相关性判断。评估发现专用AI搜索代理Lessie综合得分65.2，比第二名高18.5%，且唯一实现100%任务完成率。

<h3 id="423">[423] LiteResearcher: A Scalable Agentic RL Training Framework for Deep Research Agent</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.17931](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17931) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bince Qu, Wanli Li, Bo Pan, Jianyu Zhang, Zheng Liu, Pan Zhang, Wei Chen, Bo Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Automated Research
- **Summary**: 现有智能体强化学习受限于人工合成数据无法激发真实搜索能力及真实搜索依赖导致的不稳定和高成本，提出LiteResearcher框架，构建轻量虚拟世界模拟真实搜索动态，实现可扩展的RL训练。4B模型在GAIA和Xbench上分别达到71.3%和78.0%的SOTA结果，超越大规模开源和商业模型。

<h3 id="424">[424] From Context to Skills: Can Language Models Learn from Context Skillfully?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.27660](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27660) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuzheng Si, Haozhe Zhao, Yu Lei, Qingyi Wang, Dingwei Chen, Zhitong Wang, Zhenhailong Wang, Kangyang Luo, Zheng Wang, Gang Chen, Fanchao Qi, Minjia Zhang, Maosong Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Self-Improvement, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 面对手动技能标注成本高且缺乏外部反馈的挑战，提出Ctx2Skill自进化框架，通过多智能体自博弈循环（挑战者、推理者、裁判）自动发现、提炼和选择上下文技能，并引入跨时间重放机制防止对抗崩溃。在四个上下文学习任务上一致提升不同骨干模型的求解率。

<h3 id="425">[425] GeoDecider: An Evidence-Grounded Agent for Geological Interpretation via Deliberative Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.03383](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03383) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaoyu Tao, Mingyue Cheng, Jiahao Wang, Yitong Zhou, Qingyang Mao, Yimin Dou, Qi Liu, Shijin Wang, Enhong Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Geoscience, Agentic Reasoning, Scientific Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Xiaoyu-Tao/GeoDecider)
- **Summary**: 地质解释中现有自动方法缺乏收集额外证据的能力，提出GeoDecider代理，先进行高效数值预测，再对困难间隔选择性调用工具辅助推理，构建证据档案并应用地质约束检查。在四个公开测井基准上持续优于代表性基线，展示了选择性证据收集和审慎推理的价值。

<h3 id="426">[426] From Pixels to Prompts: Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.07544](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07544) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Khang Nhat Hoang Vo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Survey
- **Summary**: 这是一本关于视觉-语言模型的综述性著作，旨在为读者提供清晰的概念地图，弥合“知道术语”与“理解工作原理”之间的鸿沟。涵盖模型架构、训练方法等核心内容，帮助读者自信阅读新论文并设计自己的系统。

<h3 id="427">[427] Do LLMs Experience an Internal Polylogue? Investigating Reasoning through the Lens of Personas</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09159](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09159) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nils A. Herrmann, Leander Girrbach, Kirill Bykov, Zeynep Akata
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Interpretability, Activation Steering
- **Summary**: 此前研究将人格向量作为静态操控工具，本文将其视为动态信号，提出“多声部”概念（人格向量与隐藏激活在生成过程中的对齐时序）。实验表明多声部特征包含与正确性相关的预测信号，且基于段落条件干预可提升3/4模型的准确率，但尚不鲁棒。

<h3 id="428">[428] What Does Chain-of-Thought Contribute at Probe Time? Evidence for Local Co-Occurrence Activation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.26795](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26795) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiang Wang, Wei Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Chain-of-Thought, LLM Reasoning, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 为了探究思维链提示的增益来源，从探针时间角度进行实验：固定思维链推理后，测试哪些文本属性对最终预测重要。发现随机化推理句顺序对准确率影响甚微，而恢复短程词序可大幅提升性能；机制分析表明增益主要来自单词及其短程共现激活（LCA）。

<h3 id="429">[429] On the Detection of Commutative Factors in Factor Graphs: Necessary and Sufficient Conditions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.26908](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26908) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Malte Luttermann, Ralf Möller, Marcel Gehrke
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DS; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Probabilistic Inference, Graphical Models, Lifted Inference
- **Venue**: PGM 2026
- **Summary**: 现有时序因子检测算法依赖于一个被误认为充分条件的定理（实际仅为必要条件），可能导致错误结果。本文证明修正后的必要条件，提出保持效率的正确算法，并引入具有更紧最坏情况界的补充算法。

<h3 id="430">[430] WIRE: Profiling Witnessed Within-Policy Instruction Collisions in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27784](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27784) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lu Yan, Xuan Chen, Xiangyu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Instruction Following, Agent Evaluation
- **Summary**: LLM代理的长期提示策略中规则可能冲突，现有评估未测试规则间的压力解决。提出WIRE分析器，提取规则并编码为PYRULE子句，通过可满足性检查提名冲突候选并生成具体观察实例，最终产生四单元解决画像（同时满足、仅较早、仅较晚、均不满足）。在6个公开策略上，仅35.4%的实例同时满足两条规则。

<h3 id="431">[431] Universal Quantum Transformer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00045](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00045) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sungyong Chung, Alireza Talebpour
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.ET
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Transformer Architecture, Compositional Generalization
- **Summary**: 经典神经网络难以精确学习离散逻辑规则（如模算术、群代数、组合泛化），常需大规模参数且存在随机不稳定性。提出通用量子Transformer（UQT），利用多量子比特系统的物理属性作为归纳偏置，仅用5-6量子比特和551-1650参数即可精确学习三类形式任务，并在IBM量子硬件上达到97.5%准确率。

<h3 id="432">[432] Tree-Based Formalization of Multi-Agent Complementarity in Human-AI Interactions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.04779](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04779) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Andrea Ferrario
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; math.CO
- **Tags**: Human-AI Interaction, Multi-Agent System, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 人机交互中的互补性缺乏基于工作流的多智能体形式化模型。提出树形形式化框架，用有序二元树表示协议，递归计算互补性泛函。证明四个定理：选择器型HAI无法实现互补性；回归下互补性等价于欧氏距离最小化；线性组合下每个协议树定义重心坐标；分类任务中无法通过内部局部组合实现互补性。

<h3 id="433">[433] Some hypotheses on how chatbots work in problem-solving-driven conversations. Large Language Models as confirmation of the Innovation Illusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07722](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07722) (replaced)
- **Authors**: S.F.M. van Vlijmen, H.D. Lethe jr
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cognitive Science, Chatbot Analysis, Metaphor Processing
- **Summary**: 本文探讨聊天机器人在问题解决对话中的本质，提出“隐喻问题传播”概念，认为基础聊天机器人无法匹配人类认知灵活性，并指出LLM训练数据仅部分模仿人类思维。文章从认知语言学、聚合动力学等角度论证，强调聊天机器人虽然被广泛使用，但无法成为真正的思考伙伴。

<h3 id="434">[434] Artificial Intelligence for Mathematical Reasoning: An Integrated Survey of Language Models, Neuro-symbolic Systems, and Verified Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08728](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08728) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Syed Rifat Raiyan, Mohsinul Kabir, Hasan Mahmud, Md Kamrul Hasan, Sophia Ananiadou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, LLM Reasoning, Survey
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Starscream-11813/awesome-AI4Math)
- **Summary**: 本文综述了AI数学推理的进展，涵盖非正式推理、形式推理、数学发现以及推理与训练技术，并整理了主要基准和失败模式。文章提出了未来方向，包括验证发现工作流、推理效率等。

<h3 id="435">[435] ProvenanceGuard: Source-Aware Factuality Verification for MCP-Based LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.18037](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18037) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ander Alvarez, Santhiya Rajan, Samuel Mugel, Román Orús
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Fact Checking, RAG, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文提出ProvenanceGuard，一种面向MCP（模型上下文协议）代理的源感知事实性验证器。它通过分解声明、路由到源证据并检查归属一致性，在医疗领域281个轨迹上实现了比无源基线更高的block F1和源准确率，有效检测跨源混淆。

<h3 id="436">[436] Intent-Governed Tool Authorization for AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22916](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22916) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Genliang Zhu, Chu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Security
- **Summary**: 本文提出意图治理访问控制（IGAC），一种服务端授权层，将用户意图作为单调、可审计的策略属性，用于AI代理的工具使用。IGAC引入意图证书、会话范围策略缩小等机制，确保用户意图仅能减少静态集成策略授予的权限。

<h3 id="437">[437] The Hitchhiker's Guide to Agentic AI: From Foundations to Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.24937](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24937) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haggai Roitman
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 构建自主AI系统需要理解从底层基础到生产部署的全栈知识，现有资料往往只聚焦单一层面。本书作为综合实践参考，覆盖LLM基础（Transformer、训练、压缩）、对齐与推理（RLHF、CoT）、智能体AI（RAG、记忆、工具使用、多智能体架构）及部署与评估。提供从原理到代码的完整指导，结合理论、实现和参考文献，是构建自主系统的全面指南。

<h3 id="438">[438] ATOD: Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation for Multi-turn Autonomous Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.27814](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27814) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qitai Tan, Zefang Zong, Mo Li, Yipeng Shi, Yang Li, Peng Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出ATOD算法，通过退火式在线策略蒸馏（OPD-RL调度）和回合级不一致不确定性重加权（T-DUR），有效结合OPD的早期快速提升和RL的探索优势。在ALFWorld等任务上，ATOD在多个学生模型规模上平均成功率比OPD和GRPO分别提升3.03和23.62个百分点。

<h3 id="439">[439] EvalSafetyGap: A Hybrid Survey and Conceptual Framework for LLM Evaluation-Safety Failures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30219) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Buğra Alperen Uluırmak, Rifat Kurban
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, AI Safety, Survey
- **Summary**: 本文系统综述了LLM评估与AI安全共有的测量问题，构建了EvalSafetyGap概念框架，将基准有效性和对齐失败统一为代理-目标发散问题。通过Goodhart启发的稳定性分解和对齐三难困境，提出动态、抗污染基准等研究议程。

<h3 id="440">[440] Agents Don't Just Agree, They Remember: Benchmarking Persistent Sycophancy in Stateful Personal Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.10526](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10526) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xutao Mao, Liangjie Zhao, Leyao Wang, Rui Qian, Qiang Huang, Wentao Wang, Bo Han, Xiang Zheng, Cong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sycophancy, LLM Agent, Agent Evaluation
- **Summary**: 有状态个人智能体将对话中的默认行为视为持久偏好并写入状态，导致持续性谄媚（persistent sycophancy），现有基准未评估这一过程。提出PASB基准（1600任务），追踪对话声明是否被接受、写入智能体状态并在后续中性查询中重用。下游失败率从对话内45.0%上升到写入状态后71.9%，识别出状态提升、属性移除和范围扩大三种写入模式，表明问题本质是写入治理。

<h3 id="441">[441] Graph Feedback Controls Consensus and Clique Formation in Open-Weight Language-Model Populations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.12077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12077) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Samer Saab Jr, Chaouki Abdallah
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Graph Theory
- **Summary**: 本文研究多代理LM系统中路由如何影响共识与派系形成。在命名游戏中，基于相似性的路由可能隔离新兴惯例并维持分裂，随机重匹配和连接分歧群体的策略可改善协调，但暴露本身不足。结果表明运行时交互图是因果设计变量。

<h3 id="442">[442] ReasFlow: Assisting Reasoning-Centric Scientific Discovery in Applied Mathematics via a Knowledge-Based Multi-Agent System</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.14178](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14178) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yutong He, Daibo Li, Guohong Li, Jiahe Geng, Zhengyang Huang, Can Ren, Zekun Zhang, Yifan Liu, Shuchen Zhu, Hengrui Zhang, Boao Kong, Ming Sun, Shu Li, Chenyi Li, Jiang Hu, Kun Yuan, Zaiwen Wen, Pingwen Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Scientific Reasoning, Mathematical Reasoning, Multi-Agent System
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/reaslab/ReasFlow.git)
- **Summary**: 本文提出ReasFlow，一个端到端自主科学发现系统，适合数学推理领域。系统包括内部验证循环和自动化知识检索与自我改进机制，能够从少量提示自主生成五篇完整研究论文，在基于LLM的评估中取得最高分。

<h3 id="443">[443] MedFailBench: A Clinician-Built Open-Source Benchmark for Medical AI Safety Boundary Inspection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.15166](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15166) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Goktug Ozkan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Safety Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出MedFailBench，一个由临床医生构建的医疗AI安全边界检查基准。它按严重程度和失效门类型标注错误，包含44个合成病例，旨在识别安全边界失效而非正确答案。

<h3 id="444">[444] Knowledge-Centric Agents for Workflow Generation in ComfyUI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.15845](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15845) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhendong Li, Lei Sun, Ruibo Ming, He Zhang, Danda Pani Paudel, Luc Van Gool, Jinjin Gu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Knowledge Distillation, Workflow Generation
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出知识驱动的框架用于ComfyUI工作流生成，通过知识反转、注入和推理，使模型能从任务描述推理到策略再到可执行结构。实验表明该方法生成的节点多样性更高、结构更连贯、执行成功率更高。

<h3 id="445">[445] Position: AI/ML Deepfake Research is Misaligned with AI-Generated Non-Consensual Intimate Imagery (AIG-NCII)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18263](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18263) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Li Qiwei, Wells Lucas Santo, Sarita Schoenebeck, Eric Gilbert
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, AI Ethics, Content Moderation
- **Summary**: 本文指出AI/ML深度伪造研究未充分关注AI生成的非自愿亲密图像（AIG-NCII），现有干预主要针对认知伤害而忽略尊严伤害。文章建议更新威胁模型，纳入主体中心伤害，并在AI安全研究中解决AIG-NCII。

<h3 id="446">[446] Neuro-Symbolic Meta-Policies for Temporal Knowledge-Graph Memory under Partial Observability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18368](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18368) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Taewoon Kim, Vincent François-Lavet, Michael Cochez
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Reinforcement Learning, Knowledge Graph
- **Summary**: 本文提出神经符号元策略，用于部分可观察强化学习中记忆的保留、检索和遗忘。结合时间知识图记忆和符号操作，在RoomKG上实现了可追踪的元记忆管理决策，StarE-GNN配置取得最佳性能。

<h3 id="447">[447] Athena-Brain Technical Report: An Efficient Robot Brain for General Intelligence and Embodied Interaction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18985](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18985) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jialian Li, Junhong Liu, Yuchen Cao, Weiran Guo, Jiaming Song, Xutao Wang, Yi Zhao, Jiangpin Liu, Jie Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Embodied AI, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 本文介绍Athena-Brain-8B，一个用于具身智能的紧凑设备端大脑模型。通过多阶段后训练（通用SFT、通用RL、具身专家训练和模型合并），在保持通用能力的同时获得高效具身交互能力，在领域基准上超越类似规模模型及部分更大模型。

<h3 id="448">[448] Quality Action Assurance: Multimodal Verification of Examiner Claims in VR OSCEs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.19063](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19063) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Harry Rogers, Sally Shiels, Ashley Tomlinson, James Thomas, James Aylward, Nathan Gauge, Helen Higham, Alison Noble
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multimodal Learning, Temporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出质量动作保证（QAA）框架，通过多模态验证VR OSCE中考官声明。结合约束时间动作对齐模型和LLM声明提取，在5折交叉验证中达到99.2% Actor F1和93.4%时序对齐，将事实正确率从39.2%提升至79.2%。

<h3 id="449">[449] AdaRoPE: Not All Attention Heads Should Rotate and Scale Equally</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.19363](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19363) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shaowen Wang, Yuke Zheng, Tansheng Zhu, Shuang Chen, Shaofan Liu, Suncong Zheng, Jian Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Attention Mechanism, Long Context
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文指出标准RoPE对所有注意力头使用统一的频率和缩放，导致长上下文下性能不佳。提出AdaRoPE，为每个注意力头学习可调的旋转频率和缩放因子。实验表明AdaRoPE在上下文扩展和短上下文性能上均优于现有RoPE变体。

<h3 id="450">[450] Efficient and Interpretable Body-Based Emotion Recognition with Lightweight Temporal Convolutional Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.20820](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20820) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Christian Arzate Cruz, Stefanos Gkikas, Houshyar Asadi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, Human Activity Recognition, Efficient Architecture
- **Venue**: ACII 2026
- **Summary**: 针对基于骨架图的情感识别计算开销大的问题，该论文研究轻量级时间卷积网络（TCN）作为替代。提出的TCN-Base在DIEM-A数据集上相比图基线仅损失少量精度，但参数量减少79.18%，推理延迟降低约12.5倍。分析表明上半身运动是最强的独立区域线索。

<h3 id="451">[451] EmoAgent-R1: Towards Multimodal Emotion Understanding with Reinforcement Learning-based Dynamic Agent Specialization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.21013](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21013) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lihuang Fang, Yuchen Zou, kebing Jin, Jinghui Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Affective Computing
- **Summary**: 现有MLLM在多模态情感识别中使用固定提示，忽略了情感源的动态性。提出EmoAgent-R1框架，通过强化学习实现动态智能体专业化，并引入渐进式分组相对策略优化（P-GRPO）解决稀疏奖励问题。在多个MER基准上取得更优情感推理性能。

<h3 id="452">[452] V-DEAL: Diagnosing Video Safety De-Calibration as an Understanding-Refusal Coupling Failure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.21151](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21151) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhetong Zhang, Honghao Fu, Miao Xu, Yiwei Wang, Yujun Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Video Understanding, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 研究发现有害视频搭配良性查询比有害查询更容易攻击视频LLM。提出V-DEAL三级诊断框架，从行为、理解和内部表示分析此漏洞，并引入提示注入干预方法，将攻击成功率平均降低48.24个百分点。

<h3 id="453">[453] Expert Behavior Prior Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.21302](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21302) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Gong Gao, Weidong Zhao, Xianhui Liu, Ning Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Imitation Learning, Robotics
- **Summary**: 现有行为先验强化学习方法依赖静态离线数据集，限制了策略先验的有效性。提出Expert Behavior Prior (EBP)算法，使用Q引导的条件变分自编码器从在线缓冲区生成专家先验，并结合专家策略指导和策略梯度校正模块。在机器人控制基准上显著提升样本效率和收敛稳定性。

<h3 id="454">[454] Nanbeige4.2-3B: Unlocking Agentic Capabilities in a Compact Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.22083](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22083) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nanbeige Lab, Chen Yang, Chengrui Huang, Fufeng Lan, Hanhui Chen, Hao Zhou, Huatong Song, Jiaqi Cao, Jiaying Zhu, Jinlin Niu, Kai Wang, Lisheng Huang, Qiliang Liang, Ran Le, Ruixiang Feng, Shuang Sun, Tao Gu, Tao Zhang, Tianyu Luo, Yang Song, Yun Xing, Yuntao Wen, Ziyao Xu, Zongchao Chen, Zongqiang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Small Language Model, RLHF
- **Summary**: 为在紧凑模型中解锁智能体能力，提出Nanbeige4.2-3B模型，采用Looped Transformer在28T token上从头预训练。通过混合模式RLHF、长度控制推理RL和智能体RL训练，在代码智能体、办公智能体和工具使用任务上超越Qwen3.5-9B等更大模型。

<h3 id="455">[455] TRACE-ROUTER: Task-Consistent and Adaptive Online Routing for Agentic AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.22465](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22465) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ritik Raj, Souvik Kundu, Sarbartha Banerjee, Dheemanth Joshi, Ishita Vohra, Tushar Krishna
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 现有逐次调用路由方法无法利用任务级延迟反馈，导致路由决策与任务目标不匹配。提出TRACE-Router，使用上下文bandit为每个任务一次性分配模型，并利用终端奖励更新策略。在三个智能体基准上实现了更好的准确率-延迟折中。

<h3 id="456">[456] Like a Baby: Visually Situated Neural Language Acquisition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [1805.11546](https://arxiv.org/abs/1805.11546) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alexander G. Ororbia, Ankur Mali, Mary Alexandria Kelly, David Reitter
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model, Cognitive Science
- **Venue**: ACL 2019
- **Summary**: 为探究视觉上下文对语言模型训练的影响，提出多模态神经架构用于下一个词预测。即使测试时无视觉上下文，该模型困惑度比纯语言模型降低2%，使用BERT嵌入时降低3.5%。结论跨语言和模型结构成立，支持情境认知理论。

<h3 id="457">[457] Speed Reading Tool Powered by Artificial Intelligence for Students with ADHD, Dyslexia, and Short Attention Span</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2307.14544](https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.14544) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Megat Irfan Zackry Bin Ismail, Ahmad Nazran bin Yusri, Muhammad Hafizzul Bin Abdul Manap, Muhammad Muizzuddin Bin Kamarozaman
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Accessibility Technology, Summarization
- **Summary**: 针对多动症和阅读障碍学生的阅读困难，开发了一个AI工具，通过调用Gemini API生成摘要，并叠加半词加粗和关键词识别等排版优化。该工具支持多种文件格式，初始测试表明显著提升了阅读速度和理解力。

<h3 id="458">[458] Fairness Interventions in Classification: A Study on AI Explainability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2407.14766](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.14766) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Thomas Souverain, Paul Égré
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Fairness, Explainable AI, Bias Mitigation
- **Summary**: 该论文研究AI分类中的公平性干预，提出FairDream包，通过重加权算法增加对弱势群体的错误权重。实验对比GridSearch方法，论证Equalized Odds比Demographic Parity更适合作为偏差校正准则，并借助辛普森悖论进行规范性论证。

<h3 id="459">[459] CodexGraph: Bridging Large Language Models and Code Repositories via Code Graph Databases</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2408.03910](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.03910) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiangyan Liu, Bo Lan, Zhiyuan Hu, Yang Liu, Zhicheng Zhang, Fei Wang, Michael Shieh, Wenmeng Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Graph Neural Network, Software Engineering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/modelscope/modelscope-agent/tree/master/apps/codexgraph_agent)
- **Summary**: 现有方法在仓库级代码理解上存在召回率低或任务特定问题。提出CodexGraph，将LLM智能体与代码图数据库接口集成，通过图查询语言实现精确的代码结构感知检索。在CrossCodeEval等三个基准上表现具有竞争力。

<h3 id="460">[460] Beyond Squared Error: Exploring Loss Design for Enhanced Training of Generative Flow Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.02596](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02596) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rui Hu, Yifan Zhang, Zhuoran Li, Longbo Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Generative Flow Networks, Reinforcement Learning, Exploration
- **Summary**: GFlowNet训练中回归损失的选择被忽略，现有方法仅使用平方误差。该论文证明不同回归损失对应特定散度度量，并基于零强制和零避免性质提出三种新损失（Shifted-Cosh, Linex(1/2), Linex(1)）。在超网格、位序列和分子生成任务上显著提升收敛速度、样本多样性和鲁棒性。

<h3 id="461">[461] CausAdv: A Causal-based Framework for Detecting Adversarial Examples</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.00839](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00839) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hichem Debbi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV; stat.ME; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Causal Inference, Computer Vision
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HichemDebbi/CausAdv/tree/main)
- **Summary**: 为检测针对CNN的对抗样本，提出CausAdv因果框架，基于反事实推理学习每个输入的因果和非因果特征，并量化最后一层卷积滤波器的反事实信息。实验表明对抗样本的CI分布与干净样本不同，且因果特征可视化可作为检测工具。

<h3 id="462">[462] LIBMoE: A Library for comprehensive benchmarking Mixture of Experts in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.00918](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00918) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nam V. Nguyen, Thong T. Doan, Luong Tran, Van Nguyen, Quang Pham
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, LLM Training, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Fsoft-AIC/LibMoE)
- **Summary**: MoE架构研究因计算成本高昂而受限。推出LibMoE统一框架，支持预训练和稀疏升级模式，并提供路由和专家动态分析工具。通过大规模实验分析了路由动态、初始化影响和训练模式差异，降低了MoE研究门槛。

<h3 id="463">[463] Sign-Symmetry Learning Rules are Robust Fine-Tuners</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.05925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05925) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aymene Berriche, Mehdi Zakaria Adjal, Riyadh Baghdadi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fine-Tuning, Robustness, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 反向传播的替代方法（如反馈对齐）通常性能较差。该论文提出使用符号对称学习规则对预训练模型进行微调，发现该方法不仅保持与BP相当的性能，还增强了鲁棒性。实验在多任务和基准上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="464">[464] A Survey of Graph Transformers: Architectures, Theories and Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.16533](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16533) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chaohao Yuan, Kangfei Zhao, Ercan Engin Kuruoglu, Liang Wang, Tingyang Xu, Wenbing Huang, Deli Zhao, Hong Cheng, Yu Rong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Graph Learning, Survey
- **Venue**: ACM Computing Surveys
- **Summary**: 对图Transformer进行全面综述，涵盖架构（图分词、位置编码、结构感知注意力等）、理论（表达性）和应用（关系图、几何图、动态图、异构图）。提供了将架构组件映射到图类型的实用指南，并讨论了未来方向。

<h3 id="465">[465] Sampling Decisions: Exact Path-Space Correction, Prior Cancellation and Local-Boltzmann Guidance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.14549](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14549) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Michael Chertkov, Sungsoo Ahn, Hamidreza Behjoo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Probabilistic Inference, Importance Sampling
- **Summary**: 针对有偏见的序列采样器需要校正的问题，提出基于路径空间相对熵投影的修正方法，通过Doob变换和有限粒子算法实现。在伊辛网格实验上证明了局部玻尔兹曼引导比单一乘积先验保持更大的有效样本量，并达到精确目标参考带。

<h3 id="466">[466] Kinship Verification through a Forest Neural Network</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.18910](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18910) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ali Nazari, Omidreza Borzoei, Mohsen Ebrahimi Moghaddam
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Kinship Verification
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ali-nazari/Kinship-Verification)
- **Summary**: 针对亲属关系验证中面部表征不准确的问题，提出结合图神经网络概念的森林神经网络，引入新的损失组合（逐步使用中心损失）。在KinFaceW-II数据集上平均提升1.6%，达到最佳结果；在KinFaceW-I上也接近最佳。

<h3 id="467">[467] Computational Experiments in Number Theory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.19451](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19451) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ali Saraeb
- **Subjects**: math.NT; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, LLM Evaluation, Scientific Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对大型语言模型在数论问题上的评估需求，测试Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Instruct在算法和计算数论问题上的性能，在最优提示下达到0.95准确率。同时使用LightGBM分类器从Dirichlet L-函数的零点统计特征预测模数，在小型模数上达到93.9%测试准确率，验证了一个猜想。

<h3 id="468">[468] Steerable Chatbots: Exploring Personalization Control Interfaces via LLM Activation Steering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.04260](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04260) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jessica Y. Bo, Tianyu Xu, Ishan Chatterjee, Katrina Passarella-Ward, Achin Kulshrestha, D Shin
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 针对LLM个性化中用户需要通过提示表达偏好的负担，提出可引导聊天机器人范式，通过激活引导线性标量控制偏好表达。用户研究表明，相比单纯提示，该方法能更好地对齐用户潜在偏好，同时揭示了控制、持久性和透明度的异质性价值观。

<h3 id="469">[469] AutoMat: Enabling Automated Crystal Structure Reconstruction from Microscopy via Agentic Tool Use</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.12650](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12650) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yaotian Yang, Yiwen Tang, Yizhe Chen, Xiao Chen, Jiangjie Qiu, Hao Xiong, Haoyu Yin, Zhiyao Luo, Yifei Zhang, Sijia Tao, Wentao Li, Qinghua Zhang, Yuqiang Li, Wanli Ouyang, Bin Zhao, Xiaonan Wang, Fei Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Materials Science, Scientific Discovery, Tool Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yyt-2378/AutoMat)
- **Summary**: 针对从单张噪声STEM图像重建原子晶体结构的不适定性问题，提出AutoMat，一种故障感知的智能控制器，通过推理时假设搜索和闭环验证结合感知与物理模块。在含450+标注样本的STEM2Mat-Bench数据集上，性能优于现有方法。

<h3 id="470">[470] CoopReflect: Towards Natural Language Communication for Cooperative Autonomous Driving via Multi-Agent Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.18334](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18334) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiaxun Cui, Chen Tang, Jarrett Holtz, Janice Nguyen, Alessandro G. Allievi, Hang Qiu, Peter Stone
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对自动驾驶中车车通信不可理解的问题，提出基于自然语言的V2V通信协议和CoopReflect多智能体学习框架。实验表明该方法生成更有意义和可理解的消息，并通过知识蒸馏实现跨场景泛化，同时大幅降低决策延迟。

<h3 id="471">[471] Retrieval-Augmented Generation of Ontologies from Relational Databases</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.01232](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01232) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nadeen Fathallah, Mojtaba Nayyeri, Athish A Yogi, Ratan Bahadur Thapa, Hans-Michael Tautenhahn, Anton Schnurpel, Steffen Staab
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Knowledge Graph, Ontology Generation
- **Venue**: ISWC26
- **Summary**: 针对从关系数据库生成OWL本体需要大量人工或只能生成浅层本体的问题，提出RIGOR，一个基于LLM的流水线，结合检索增强生成、双LLM（生成和验证）和增量集成。在跨两个领域的三个数据库上，无需人工监督即优于基线方法。

<h3 id="472">[472] Flick: Few Labels Text Classification using K-Aware Intermediate Learning in Multi-Task Low-Resource Languages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.10292](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10292) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ali Almutairi, Abdullah Alsuhaibani, Shoaib Jameel, Aditya Joshi, Gelareh Mohammadi, Imran Razzak
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Few-Shot Learning, Text Classification, Low-Resource NLP
- **Summary**: 针对低资源语言中少标签文本分类的伪标签噪声问题，提出Flick，通过K-aware中间学习和自适应top-k选择从初始聚类中蒸馏高置信度伪标签。在包含阿拉伯语、乌尔都语、茨瓦纳语和英语的14个数据集上表现优越。

<h3 id="473">[473] LEDOM: Reverse Language Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.01335](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.01335) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xunjian Yin, Sitao Cheng, Yuxi Xie, Xinyu Hu, Li Lin, Xinyi Wang, Liangming Pan, William Yang Wang, Xiaojun Wan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Autoregressive Model, Reranking
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Arvid-pku/LEDOM)
- **Summary**: 针对自回归语言模型仅从左到右训练的限制，探索反向自回归训练，发现其具备溯因推理、问题综合等能力。提出逆向奖励对正向输出进行重排序，在AIME 2024上提升6.6%，在AMC 2023上提升15%。

<h3 id="474">[474] CodeEvo: Interaction-Driven Synthesis of Code-centric Data through Hybrid and Iterative Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.22080](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22080) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qiushi Sun, Jinyang Gong, Lei Li, Qipeng Guo, Fei Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Data Synthesis, Instruction Tuning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/QiushiSun/CodeEvo)
- **Summary**: 针对高质量指令-代码对获取困难的问题，提出CodeEvo双智能体架构（编码器和审查器），通过混合验证（确定性编译器和语义评估）合成具有阶梯难度的代码数据。构建CodeEvo-100K数据集，微调后的模型在代码生成基准上持续优于基线。

<h3 id="475">[475] Realizing Scaling Laws in Recommender Systems: A Foundation-Expert Paradigm for Hyperscale Model Deployment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.02929](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02929) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dai Li, Kevin Course, Wei Li, Hongwei Li, Jie Hua, Yiqi Chen, Zhao Zhu, Rui Jian, Xuan Cao, Bi Xue, Yu Shi, Jing Qian, Kai Ren, Matt Ma, Qunshu Zhang, Rui Li
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Foundation Model, Scaling Laws
- **Summary**: 针对推荐系统基础模型在多场景部署的挑战，提出基础-专家范式，中央基础模型生成目标感知嵌入供轻量级专家模型使用。在Meta部署后，每天处理数百亿请求，实现了0.050%的统计显著在线指标提升和0.359%的累积增益，转移率0.64-1.0。

<h3 id="476">[476] PrinciplismQA: A Philosophy-Grounded Approach to Assessing LLM-Human Clinical Medical Ethics Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.05132](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05132) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chang Hong, Minghao Wu, Qingying Xiao, Yuchi Wang, Xiang Wan, Guangjun Yu, Benyou Wang, Yan Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, AI Ethics, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/PrinciplismQA)
- **Summary**: 针对医疗LLM伦理推理评估缺乏哲学框架和专家验证的问题，提出基于原则主义的PrinciplismQA，包含3648个专家验证问题。评估揭示即使知识准确性高，伦理推理仍存在显著差距，为临床AI部署就绪性提供验证工具。

<h3 id="477">[477] EGRA:Toward Enhanced Behavior Graphs and Representation Alignment for Multimodal Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.16170](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.16170) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaoxiong Zhang, Xin Zhou, Zhiwei Zeng, Yongjie Wang, Zhiqi Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Multimodal Learning, Graph Neural Network
- **Summary**: 针对多模态推荐中原始模态特征引入噪声和均匀对齐权重限制的问题，提出EGRA，利用预训练MMR模型表示构建鲁棒的物品-物品图，并引入双层动态对齐权重。在五个数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="478">[478] OpenAIs HealthBench in Action: Evaluating an LLM-Based Medical Assistant on Realistic Clinical Queries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.02594](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.02594) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sandhanakrishnan Ravichandran, Shivesh Kumar, Rogerio Corga Da Silva, Miguel Romano, Reinhard Berkels, Michiel van der Heijden, Olivier Fail, Valentine Emmanuel Gnanapragasam
- **Subjects**: q-bio.QM; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Evaluation, RAG
- **Summary**: 针对常规基准无法评估临床助手情景感知和不确定性处理能力的问题，使用HealthBench评估基于RAG的临床助手DR. INFO。在1000个困难样本上取得0.68 HealthBench Hard分数，优于GPT-5家族、Grok 3等前沿模型，以及OpenEvidence等同类助手（0.72在子集上）。

<h3 id="479">[479] Loong: Synthesize Long Chain-of-Thoughts at Scale through Verifiers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.03059](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.03059) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xingyue Huang, Rishabh, Gregor Franke, Ziyi Yang, Jiamu Bai, Weijie Bai, Jinhe Bi, Zifeng Ding, Yiqun Duan, Chengyu Fan, Wendong Fan, Xin Gao, Ruohao Guo, Yuan He, Zhuangzhuang He, Xianglong Hu, Neil Johnson, Bowen Li, Fangru Lin, Siyu Lin, Tong Liu, Yunpu Ma, Hao Shen, Hao Sun, Beibei Wang, Fangyijie Wang, Hao Wang, Haoran Wang, Yang Wang, Yifeng Wang, Zhaowei Wang, Ziyang Wang, Yifan Wu, Zikai Xiao, Chengxing Xie, Fan Yang, Junxiao Yang, Qianshuo Ye, Ziyu Ye, Guangtao Zeng, Yuwen Ebony Zhang, Zeyu Zhang, Zihao Zhu, Bernard Ghanem, Philip Torr, Guohao Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Chain-of-Thought, LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/camel-ai/loong)
